- **test-driven-development**: Use this skill when writing or changing code whose correctness matters and you want proof it works — not a claim that it works. The core move is RED → GREEN → REFACTOR: write a failing test, make it pass, then clean up. This is the methodology; webapp-testing and run_tests are the tools.
